=== A1 STRUCTURED EXTRACTION ===
source: LT_Q2FY27_Results.pdf (33 pages); fulltext: runs/lt-q2fy27/extracted/lt-results-q2fy27-fulltext.md (line numbers cite that file)
company: Larsen & Toubro Limited (LT) | quarter: Q2 FY27 (quarter and six months ended 30-Sep-2026) | doctype: results
unit_convention: Rs Crore (conversion factor to Rs Crores x1); values kept verbatim, nothing converted. Page 9 uses Indian digit grouping (1,31,662); statements use plain decimals.
ROW ID RANGE: R001-R2511
COUNTS: NUMBER 1884 | ENTITY 239 | FORWARD 16 | DATE 231 | QUALIFIER 141 | TOTAL 2511
ROW FORMAT: R### | page N | line L | TYPE | verbatim value | context [| FLAGS]
FLAGS: ZERO_STANDING (nil/dash value), NOT_APPLICABLE (NA), PARTIAL_COLS(right-aligned), RECONSTRUCTED_FROM_RENDER (page 14 values transcribed from render), FOOTNOTE_MARKER / FOOTNOTE_MARKER_OCR / FOOTNOTE_UNRESOLVED, WRITTEN_NUMBER.
COLUMN KEY (statement NUMBER rows name the period in context): Qtr 30-Sep-2026 | Qtr 30-Jun-2026 | Qtr 30-Sep-2025 | 6M 30-Sep-2026 | 6M 30-Sep-2025 | FY 31-Mar-2026 (six-column tables); 30-Sep-2026 | 31-Mar-2026 (balance sheets); 6M columns for cash flows.
ATOMICITY: one row per (metric x period) value; every statement table cell is its own row (consolidated and standalone P&L, balance sheets, cash flows, ratios, segment revenue/results/assets/liabilities, Annexure 1 group and segment tables).
SCOPE NOTES (mechanical): (1) Regulatory-reference numbers (Regulation/Section/Ind AS/SRE/ISO numbers), CIN, phone, postal codes, page footers and table serial numbers are not NUMBER rows; the identifiers that matter (ISIN, firm registration, membership) are ENTITY rows. (2) Annexure A consolidation lists (pages 24-28, 31) are one ENTITY row per entity, category and serial in context (results filing: no grouping). (3) Page 14 values are from the render because the text layer detached them from labels; other OCR corrections are listed in the fulltext header VERIFY_LOG. (4) Page 15 segment notes (I)-(VI) have OCR spelling damage in the text layer; captured as extracted. (5) Audit report numbers on pages 22, 23, 30 had OCR-spaced decimals (e.g. "590. 77"); normalised to 590.77 etc. in NUMBER values; fulltext keeps the source spacing. (6) Press-release and statement tables repeat the same facts; each occurrence is its own row.
